Fight Night: Machida vs. Muñoz]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 166: Velasquez vs. dos Santos III''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on October 19, 2013, at the [[Toyota Center]] in [[Houston, Texas]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/6/13/4427854/cain-velasquez-vs-junior-dos-santos-3-trilogy-fight-announced-for-ufc-166-in-october-mma|title=Cain Velasquez vs. Junior dos Santos 3 trilogy fight announced for UFC 166 in October|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2013-06-13|access-date=2013-06-13}}\n\n==Background==\nThe main event was a [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Cain Velasquez]] and top contender [[Junior dos Santos]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/9442556/cain-velasquez-junior-dos-santos-meet-3rd|title=Velasquez, dos Santos To Fight For Third Time|publisher=espn.go.com.|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2013-07-01|access-date=2013-07-01}} The two have split their two previous encounters with Dos Santos winning the first bout via first round knockout at [[UFC on Fox: Velasquez vs. dos Santos]] and then Velasquez winning the rematch via unanimous decision at [[UFC 155]].\n\nA bout between [[Tony Ferguson]] and [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] was rescheduled from [[UFC 164]] however visa complications left Nurmagomedov unable to compete and was subsequently replaced by [[Mike Rio]].\n\nA bout between [[George Roop]] and [[Francisco Rivera (fighter)|Francisco Rivera]] was scheduled for this card, but was moved to [[UFC Fight Night 31]] and replaced by [[Sarah Kaufman (fighter)|Sarah Kaufman]] vs. [[Jessica Eye]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/ufc-166-adds-eye-vs-kaufman-sends-rivera-vs-roop-to-ufc-fight-night-31|title=UFC 166 adds Eye vs. Kaufman, sends Rivera vs. Roop to UFC Fight Night 31|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-08-23|access-date=2013-08-23|archive-date=2013-08-24|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130824000820/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/ufc-166-adds-eye-vs-kaufman-sends-rivera-vs-roop-to-ufc-fight-night-31|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Matt Grice]] was expected to face [[Jeremy Larsen]] at the event. However, Grice was forced out of the bout after sustaining serious injuries in a September 8 car crash in his home state of Oklahoma.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/ufcs-matt-grice-hospitalized-after-overnight-car-accident|title=UFC's Matt Grice hospitalized after overnight car accident|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-09|access-date=2013-09-09|archive-date=2013-09-11|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130911073316/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/ufcs-matt-grice-hospitalized-after-overnight-car-accident|url-status=dead}} Grice was replaced by [[Charles Oliveira]] who also lost his original opponent, Estevan Payan, due to injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/with-estevan-payan-out-charles-oliveira-meets-jeremy-larsen-at-ufc-166|title=With Estevan Payan out, Charles Oliveira meets Jeremy Larsen at UFC 166|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-10|access-date=2013-09-10|archive-date=2013-09-13|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130913011648/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/with-estevan-payan-out-charles-oliveira-meets-jeremy-larsen-at-ufc-166|url-status=dead}} Subsequently on October 5, Oliveira pulled out of the Larsen bout citing a strained thigh muscle.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/10/5/4805686/charles-oliveira-injured-removed-ufc-166-fight-card-houston-mma|title=Charles Oliveira injured, removed from UFC 166 fight card in Houston|publisher=MMAMania.com|author=Thomas Myers|date=2013-10-05|access-date=2013-10-05}} Larsen instead faced promotional newcomer [[Andre Fili]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/ufc-signs-andre-fili-to-meet-jeremy-larsen-at-ufc-166-in-houston|title=UFC signs Andre Fili to meet Jeremy Larsen at UFC 166 in Houston|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-10-07|access-date=2013-10-07|archive-date=2013-10-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131007211110/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/ufc-signs-andre-fili-to-meet-jeremy-larsen-at-ufc-166-in-houston|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Luke Rockhold]] was expected to face [[Tim Boetsch]] at the event. However, Rockhold was forced out of the bout citing a knee injury and was replaced by [[C. B. Dollaway]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/luke-rockhold-out-c-b-dollaway-meets-tim-boetsch-at-ufc-166|title=Luke Rockhold out, C. B. Dollaway meets Tim Boetsch at UFC 166|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-18|access-date=2013-09-18|archive-date=2013-09-22|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130922040955/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/luke-rockhold-out-c-b-dollaway-meets-tim-boetsch-at-ufc-166|url-status=dead}}\n\nAndre Fili weighed in 2.5 pounds too heavy. As a result, 20-percent of Fili's purse was forfeited to his opponent, [[Jeremy Larsen]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2013/10/dana-white-andre-fili-gets-a-pass-for-missing-weight-for-ufc-166|title=Dana White: Andre Fili gets a pass for missing weight for UFC 166|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-10-20|access-date=2013-10-20}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Cain Velasquez]] (c)\n| def.\n|[[Junior dos Santos]]\n|TKO (slam and punch)\n|5\n|3:09\n|For the [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Daniel Cormier]]\n|def.\n|[[Roy Nelson (fighter)|Roy Nelson]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight\n|[[Gilbert Melendez]]\n|def.\n|[[Diego Sanchez]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 30–27, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Gabriel Gonzaga]]\n|def.\n|[[Shawn Jordan]]\n|KO (punches)\n|1\n|1:33\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Flyweight\n|[[John Dodson (fighter)|John Dodson]]\n|def.\n|[[Darrell Montague]]\n|KO (punch)\n|1\n|4:13\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary card (Fox Sports 1)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Tim Boetsch]]\n|def.\n|[[C. Hendricks''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on November 16, 2013, at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]], U.S.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/ufc-167-st-pierre-vs-Hendricks-heads-to-vegas-mgm-grand-garden-arena|title=UFC 167, St-Pierre vs. Hendricks heads to Vegas' MGM Grand Garden Arena|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-06-15|access-date=2013-06-15|archive-date=2018-10-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20181008095912/https://mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/ufc-167-st-pierre-vs-Hendricks-heads-to-vegas-mgm-grand-garden-arena|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event commemorated the organization's 20th anniversary. The main event was a [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between the champion [[Georges St-Pierre]] and #1 contender [[Johny Hendricks]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/georges-st-pierre-vs-johny-Hendricks-slotted-for-nov-16-likely-as-ufc-167|title=Georges St-Pierre vs. Johny Hendricks slotted for Nov. 16, likely as UFC 167|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-06-19|access-date=2013-06-19|archive-date=2013-06-22|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130622064256/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/06/georges-st-pierre-vs-johny-hendricks-slotted-for-nov-16-likely-as-ufc-167|url-status=dead}} Co-featured on the card was a light-heavyweight bout between [[Rashad Evans]] and [[Chael Sonnen]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/rashad-evans-vs-chael-sonnen-set-for-ufc-167-at-light-heavyweight|title=Rashad Evans vs. Chael Sonnen set for UFC 167 at light heavyweight|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2013-09-04|archive-date=2013-09-06|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130906042758/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/rashad-evans-vs-chael-sonnen-set-for-ufc-167-at-light-heavyweight|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Rafael Natal]] was briefly scheduled to face [[Ed Herman]] at the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.fightline.com/ufc-167-adds-ed-herman-rafael-natal-erik-perez-edwin-figueroa/|title=UFC 167 Adds Ed Herman-Rafael Natal|publisher=fightline.com|author=Dana Becker|date=September 16, 2013|access-date=October 2, 2013|archive-date=November 9, 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131109010202/http://www.fightline.com/ufc-167-adds-ed-herman-rafael-natal-erik-perez-edwin-figueroa/|url-status=dead}} However, Natal was pulled from the pairing with Herman in favor of a bout with [[Tim Kennedy (fighter)|Tim Kennedy]] on November 6, 2013, in the event headliner at [[UFC Fight Night 31]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/tim-kennedy-gets-rafael-natal-at-ufcs-fight-for-the-troops-3|title=Tim Kennedy gets Rafael Natal at UFC's 'Fight for the Troops 3'|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=October 1, 2013|archive-date=October 1, 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131001223442/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/tim-kennedy-gets-rafael-natal-at-ufcs-fight-for-the-troops-3|url-status=dead}} Herman instead faced [[Thales Leites]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/thales-leites-replaces-rafael-natal-faces-ed-herman-at-ufc-167|title=Thales Leites replaces Rafael Natal, faces Ed Herman at UFC 167|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=October 2, 2013|archive-date=October 2, 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131002235531/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/thales-leites-replaces-rafael-natal-faces-ed-herman-at-ufc-167|url-status=dead}}\n\nA bout between [[Frank Mir]] and [[Alistair Overeem]] was scheduled for this card, but was moved to [[UFC 169]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jon-jones-glover-teixeira-mir-overeem-top-ufc-169-on-super-bowl-weekend|title=Jon Jones-Glover Teixeira, Mir-Overeem top UFC 169 on Super Bowl weekend|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=October 3, 2013|archive-date=October 3, 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131003234646/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jon-jones-glover-teixeira-mir-overeem-top-ufc-169-on-super-bowl-weekend|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Robert Drysdale]] was initially scheduled to face [[Cody Donovan]] on this card. However, Drysdale was refused a fighting license by the Nevada State Athletic Commission (NSAC) after an out-of-competition drug test revealed that he had an elevated testosterone-to-epitestosterone ratio of 19.4.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/robert-drysdale-denied-ufc-167-license-after-testing-for-elevated-te-ratio|title=Robert Drysdale denied UFC 167 license due to elevated T/E ratio|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2013-10-28|archive-date=2013-10-30|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131030020721/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/robert-drysdale-denied-ufc-167-license-after-testing-for-elevated-te-ratio|url-status=dead}} Donovan instead faced [[Gian Villante]].\n\n[[Vaughan Lee (fighter)|Vaughan Lee]] was expected to face [[Sergio Pettis]] at this event. However, Lee had to pull out citing an undisclosed injury and was replaced by [[Will Campuzano]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/11/will-campuzano-returns-to-ufc-meets-sergio-pettis-at-ufc-167|title=Will Campuzano returns to UFC, meets Sergio Pettis at UFC '''Fight of The Night: Georges St-Pierre vs. Johny Hendricks'''\n* '''Knockout of The Night: Tyron Woodley'''\n* '''Submission of the Night: Donald Cerrone'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/11/18/91526/|title=UFC 167 salaries: Georges St-Pierre, Rashad Evans top $1,641,000 payroll|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2013-11-18|access-date=2013-11-18|author=Staff|archive-date=2013-11-19|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131119232316/http://mmajunkie.com/2013/11/18/91526/|url-status=dead}}\n* '''Georges St-Pierre:''' $400,000 (no win bonus) def. = [[UFC Fight Night: Saffiedine vs. Lim]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 168: Weidman vs. Silva 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on December 28, 2013, at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/weidman-vs-silva-rematch-heads-ufc-168-on-dec-28-rousey-vs-tate-ii-co-main|title=Weidman vs. Silva rematch heads UFC 168 on Dec. 28, Rousey vs. Tate II co-main|work=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=July 13, 2013|access-date=July 13, 2013|archive-date=November 28, 2020|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201128090411/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/weidman-vs-silva-rematch-heads-ufc-168-on-dec-28-rousey-vs-tate-ii-co-main|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] rematch between current champion [[Chris Weidman]] and former champion [[Anderson Silva]]. The two previously met at [[UFC 162]], with Weidman winning via KO in the second round and earning the title.\n\nCo-featured was a [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Ronda Rousey]] and [[Miesha Tate]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/ronda-rousey-vs-miesha-tate-ii-slated-for-year-end-ufc-168-pay-per-view|title=Ronda Rousey vs. Miesha Tate II slated for year-end UFC 168 pay-per-view|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=July 2, 2013|access-date=July 2, 2013|archive-date=July 5, 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130705013149/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/ronda-rousey-vs-miesha-tate-ii-slated-for-year-end-ufc-168-pay-per-view|url-status=dead}} The two met before under the [[Strikeforce: Tate vs. Rousey|Strikeforce]] banner in 2012 with Rousey winning via first round submission. This served as the coaches bout for [[The Ultimate Fighter 18]].\n\nInitially, it was announced that Rousey would face top contender [[Cat Zingano]] at this event, after Zingano defeated Tate at [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen#The Ultimate Fighter 17 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter 17 Finale]].{{cite web|url=http://topmmanews.com/2013/03/16/rousey-to-coach-tuf-18-menwomen-bantamweights-to-compete/|title=Rousey to Coach TUF 18; Men/Women Bantamweights to Compete|publisher=TopMMANews.com|date=March 16, 2013|access-date=March 16, 2013|author=Keith Grienke}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/4/14/4223804/tuf-17-finale-aftermath-ronda-rousey-vs-cat-zingano-will-be-a-monster|title=TUF 17 Finale Aftermath: Ronda Rousey vs. Cat Zingano will be a monster |publisher=mmafighting.com|date=April 14, 2013|access-date=April 14, 2013|author=Dave Doyle}} However, Zingano was pulled from the bout, and subsequently as coach for The Ultimate Fighter, after it was announced that she had suffered a knee injury and would be sidelined for several months. Tate was tabbed as the replacement and the rematch with Rousey was announced.{{cite web|url=http://msn.foxsports.com/ufc/story/miesha-tate-replacing-cat-zingano-the-ultimate-fighter-ronda-rousey-fox-sports-1-052813|title=Tate replacing Zingano on TUF|publisher=msn.foxsports.com.|date=May 28, 2013|access-date=May 28, 2013|author=A.J. Perez}}\n\n[[Shane del Rosario]] was expected to face [[Guto Inocente]] at the event, but both men pulled out of the bout, citing injuries. On November 26, 2013, del Rosario had two sudden heart attacks and died on December 9, 2013.{{cite web|url=http://uk.eurosport.yahoo.com/news/ufc-168-loses-shane-del-000143814--mma.html|title=UFC 168 Loses Shane del Rosario vs. Guto Inocente Due to Injuries|publisher=uk.eurosport.yahoo.com|date=November 22, 2013|access-date=November 22, 2013|author=Staff}}{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Heavyweight-Shane-del-Rosario-in-Critical-Condition-After-Suffering-Cardiac-Arrest-59989|title=Updated: UFC Heavyweight Shane del Rosario in Critical Condition After Suffering Cardiac Arrest|publisher=sherdog.com|date=November 27, 2013|access-date=November 27, 2013|author=Tristen Critchfield}}\n\n[[Diego Brandão]] weighed in seven pounds over the featherweight limit of 146 lb. Brandão re-weighed and was 151.5, but was still fined 25% of his purse for missing weight.{{Cite web | url = http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/12/27/5249748/ufc-168-diego-brandao-fined-missing-weight-fight-dustin-poirier-still-on-mma-news | publisher = Bloody Elbow | access-date = December 31, 2013 | title = UFC 168: Diego Brandao fined 25% for missing weight, fight with Dustin Poirier still on | first = Mookie | last = Alexander | date = December 27, 2013 | archive-date = December 30, 2013 | archive-url }}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Chris Weidman]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Anderson Silva]]\n|TKO (leg injury)\n|2\n|1:16\n|For the [[UFC However, Caraway pulled out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by UFC newcomer [[Aljamain Sterling]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmaweekly.com/ufc-newcomer-aljamain-sterling-replaces-bryan-caraway-faces-lucas-martins-at-ufc-170|title=UFC Newcomer Aljamain Sterling Replaces Bryan Caraway, Faces Lucas Martins at UFC 170 |publisher=MMAweekly.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-01-29}} Martins ended up also getting injured and was replaced by UFC newcomer [[Cody Gibson]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-170-Card-Complete|title=UFC 170 Card Complete|publisher=UFC.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2014-02-11|date=2014-02-11}}\n\n[[Francisco Rivera (fighter)|Francisco Rivera]] was expected to face [[Raphael Assunção]] on this card. However, Rivera injured his hand and was forced out of the bout.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/broken-hand-knocks-francisco-rivera-from-ufc-170-fight-with-raphael-assuncao/|title=Broken hand knocks Francisco Rivera from UFC 170 fight with Raphael Assuncao|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=February 3, 2014|access-date=February 3, 2014|archive-date=February 3, 2014|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140203214716/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/broken-hand-knocks-francisco-rivera-from-ufc-170-fight-with-raphael-assuncao/|url-status=dead}} He was replaced by UFC newcomer [[Pedro Munhoz]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2014/2/9/5395712/ufc-signs-pedro-munhoz-vs-raphael-assuncao-ufc-170-bantamweigth-mma-news|title=Report: UFC signs Pedro Munhoz to fight Raphael Assuncao at UFC 170|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Zane Simon|access-date=2014-02-09|date=2014-02-09}}\n\nA bout between [[Rafael dos Anjos]] and [[Rustam Khabilov]] was expected to take place on this card. However, the fight was cancelled due to injury.{{cite web|url=https://twitter.com/UFCNews/status/430911024108015616|title=Bah – Rustam Khabilov VS Rafael dos Anjos has been cancelled from UFC 170 due to injury|publisher=Twitter|author=UFC News|access-date=2014-02-04|date=2014-02-04}}\n\nA bout between [[Rashad Evans]] and [[Daniel Cormier]] was expected to take place on this card. [[UFC 166|UFC 166: Velasquez vs. dos Santos III]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC: Fight for the Troops 3]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Machida vs. Muñoz''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 30''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on October 26, 2013, at the [[Manchester Arena|Phones 4u Arena]] in [[Manchester]], [[United Kingdom]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/8/3/4586146/mma-news-michael-bisping-vs-mark-munoz-ufc-manchester-october-26th|title=Michael Bisping vs. Mark Muñoz to headline UFC on Fox Sports 2 show in Manchester on October 26th|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2013-08-03|access-date=2013-08-03|archive-date=5 August 2013|archive-url=https://archive.today/20130805165445/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2013/8/3/4586146/mma-news-michael-bisping-vs-mark-munoz-ufc-manchester-october-26th|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\nThe main card of the event was broadcast on [[Fox Sports 2]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/manchesters-ufc-fight-night-30-to-remain-on-fox-sports-2|title=Manchester's UFC Fight Night 30 to remain on FOX Sports 2|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-04|access-date=2013-09-04|archive-date=6 September 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130906042737/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/manchesters-ufc-fight-night-30-to-remain-on-fox-sports-2|url-status=dead}}\n\nThe event was expected to be headlined by a middleweight bout between top contenders [[Michael Bisping]] and [[Mark Muñoz]]. However, on September 27 Bisping pulled out of the bout with an [[Retinal detachment|eye injury]] and was replaced by former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Lyoto Machida]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/michael-bisping-out-at-ufc-fight-night-30-lyoto-machida-now-faces-mark-munoz|title=Michael Bisping out at UFC Fight Night 30, Lyoto Machida now faces Mark Muñoz|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-27|access-date=2013-09-27|archive-date=27 September 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130927224902/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/michael-bisping-out-at-ufc-fight-night-30-lyoto-machida-now-faces-mark-munoz|url-status=dead}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/9/29/4783680/michael-bisping-apologizes-to-fans-for-eye-injury-that-forced-him|title=Michael Bisping apologizes to fans for eye injury that forced him from bout with Mark Muñoz |publisher=mmamania.com|author=Matthew Roth|date=2013-09-29|access-date=2013-09-29}}\n\n[[Tom Watson (fighter)|Tom Watson]] was expected to face [[Alessio Sakara]] at this event. However, Watson had to pull out due to an injury and was replaced by [[Magnus Cedenblad]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/tom-watson-out-magnus-cedenblad-meets-alessio-sakara-at-ufc-fight-night-31|title=Tom Watson out, Magnus Cedenblad meets Alessio Sakara at UFC Fight Night 30|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-15|access-date=2013-09-15|archive-date=17 September 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130917003525/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/tom-watson-out-magnus-cedenblad-meets-alessio-sakara-at-ufc-fight-night-31|url-status=dead}} Subsequently, in early October, Cedenblad was forced out of the Sakara bout with an injury and replaced by newcomer [[Nico Musoke]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/nicholas-musoke-meets-alessio-sakara-at-ufc-fight-night-30-in-manchester|title=Nicholas Musoke meets Alessio Sakara at UFC Fight Night 30 in Manchester|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-10-10|access-date=2013-10-10|archive-date=10 October 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131010131327/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/nicholas-musoke-meets-alessio-sakara-at-ufc-fight-night-30-in-manchester|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Paul Taylor (fighter)|Paul Taylor]] was expected to face [[Anthony Njokuani]] at this event. However, Taylor was forced to pull out due to an injury and was replaced by [[Al Iaquinta]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/al-iaquinta-replaces-paul-taylor-faces-anthony-njokuani-at-ufc-fight-night-30|title=Al Iaquinta replaces Paul Taylor, faces Anthony Njokuani at UFC Fight Night 30|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-19|access-date=2013-09-19|archive-date=21 September 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130921060645/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/al-iaquinta-replaces-paul-taylor-faces-anthony-njokuani-at-ufc-fight-night-30|url-status=dead}} On September 24, Njokuani also pulled out of the event with an injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/injury-knocks-anthony-njokuani-out-of-ufc-fight-night-30-bout-with-al-iaquinta|title=Injury knocks Anthony Njokuani out of UFC Fight Night 30 bout with Al Iaquinta|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-24|access-date=2013-09-24|archive-date=25 September 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130925170507/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/injury-knocks-anthony-njokuani-out-of-ufc-fight-night-30-bout-with-al-iaquinta|url-status=dead}} Iaquinta eventually faced [[Piotr Hallmann]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/piotr-hallman-replaces-njokuani-meets-al-iaquinta-at-ufc-fight-night-30|title=Piotr Hallman replaces Njokuani, meets Al Iaquinta at UFC Fight Night 30|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-26|access-date=2013-09-26|archive-date=26 September 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130926225210/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/piotr-hallman-replaces-njokuani-meets-al-iaquinta-at-ufc-fight-night-30|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Mike Wilkinson (fighter)|Mike Wilkinson]] was expected to face [[Jimy Hettes]] at the event. However, Wilkinson pulled out of the bout in the week leading up the bout and was replaced by newcomer [[Rob Whiteford]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/robert-whiteford-expected-to-face-jimy-hettes-at-ufc-fight-night-30|title=Robert Whiteford expected to face Jimy Hettes at UFC Fight Night 30|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=John Morgan and Matt Erickson|date=2013-10-20|access-date=2013-10-20|archive-date=20 October 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131020125914/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/robert-whiteford-expected-to-face-jimy-hettes-at-ufc-fight-night-30|url-status=dead}}{{cite web |url=http://www.mmamount.com/news/2013/10/ufc-lands-first-scottish-fighter-robert-whiteford-to-face-jimy-hettes/|title=UFC lands first Scottish fighter, Robert Whiteford to face Jimy Hettes|publisher=MMAmount.com|author=Staff|date=2013-10-20|access-date=2013-10-23}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins [[John Lineker]] weighed in at 128 pounds, two pounds over the non-title fight limit. He was fined a percentage of his purse which went to his opponent [[Phil Harris (fighter)|Phil Harris]].{{cite web |url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2013/10/25/5027768/ufc-fight-night-30-weigh-in-results-machida-munoz-make-weight/|title=UFC Fight Night 30 weigh-in results: Machida, Munoz make weight|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Chuck Mindenhall |date=2013-10-25|access-date=2013-10-25}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 2)}}\n{{MMAevent {{cite news|url=http://mmapayout.com/blue-book/pay-per-view/|title=Pay-per-view|publisher=mmapayout.com|date=June 6, 2014|access-date=September 13, 2014|archive-date=June 27, 2011|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20110627142203/http://mmapayout.com/blue-book/pay-per-view/|url-status=dead}}\n|gate= $1.7 million\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Brown vs. Silva]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Muñoz vs. Mousasi]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 173: Barão vs. Dillashaw''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held at the [[MGM Grand Garden Arena]] in [[Las Vegas, Nevada]], on May 24, 2014.\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Middleweight Championship|Middleweight Championship]] bout between then champion [[Chris Weidman]] and number one contender [[Vitor Belfort]] was scheduled to headline this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/chris-weidman-vs-vitor-belfort-title-fight-slated-for-ufc-173-in-may/|title=Chris Weidman vs. Vitor Belfort title fight slated for UFC 173 in May|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|access-date=2014-02-01|date=2014-02-01|archive-date=2014-02-02|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140202171816/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/chris-weidman-vs-vitor-belfort-title-fight-slated-for-ufc-173-in-may/|url-status=dead}} However, on February 27, the [[Nevada Athletic Commission|Nevada State Athletic Commission]] officially banned [[Androgen replacement therapy|testosterone replacement therapy]] (TRT) from combat sports. Belfort, who was using such therapy, was forced to withdraw from the bout and stated, \"Given the time constraints involved between now and my proposed next bout in May, I have determined not to apply for a license to fight in Nevada at this time.\"\n\nThe main event was quickly changed as former UFC Light Heavyweight Champion [[Lyoto Machida]] agreed to step in to face Weidman for the title. However, on March 24, the UFC announced that Weidman had suffered a knee injury and that a new headline bout would be found. The Weidman title defense against Machida was moved to [[UFC 175]] in July.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-173-update-weidman|title=Weidman out of UFC 173 with injury |author=UFC |publisher=UFC.com|access-date=2014-03-24|date=2014-03-24}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/injury-moves-chris-weidman-lyoto-machida-title-fight-from-ufc-173-to-ufc-175/|title=Injury moves Chris Weidman-Lyoto Machida title fight from UFC 173 to UFC 175|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|access-date=2014-03-24|date=2014-03-24|archive-date=2014-03-25|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140325084007/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/injury-moves-chris-weidman-lyoto-machida-title-fight-from-ufc-173-to-ufc-175/|url-status=dead}}\n\nAfter the announcement that Weidman/Machida bout had been moved, a [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|Bantamweight Championship]] bout between [[Renan Barão]] and [[Raphael Assunção]] was targeted as the event headliner. However, Assunção opted to decline the bout as a rib injury sustained in his last bout at [[UFC 170]] had not healed enough to resume the proper training in advance of the event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/raphael-assuncao-explains-why-he-isnt-fighting-renan-barao-at-ufc-173/|title=Raphael Assuncao explains why he isn't fighting Renan Barao at UFC 173|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-03-28|date=2014-03-28|archive-date=2014-03-28|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140328203854/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/raphael-assuncao-explains-why-he-isnt-fighting-renan-barao-at-ufc-173/|url-status=dead}} Barão instead fought [[T.J. Dillashaw]] in the main event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/ufc-bantamweight-champ-renan-barao-faces-t-j-dillashaw-in-new-ufc-173-headliner/|title=UFC bantamweight champ Renan Barao faces T.J. Dillashaw in new UFC 173 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|access-date=2014-03-27|date=2014-03-27|archive-date=2019-04-10|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20190410111322/https://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/ufc-bantamweight-champ-renan-barao-faces-t-j-dillashaw-in-new-ufc-173-headliner|url-status=dead}} As a result, Dillashaw's original opponent at the event, [[Takeya Mizugaki]], faced [[Francisco Rivera (fighter)|Francisco Rivera]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/francisco-rivera-steps-in-to-fight-takeya-mizugaki-at-ufc-173/|title=Francisco Rivera steps in to fight Takeya Mizugaki at UFC 173|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|access-date=2014-03-28|date=2014-03-28}}\n\nThe originally scheduled co-main event was [[Junior dos Santos]] against [[Stipe Miocic]].{{cite web|url=http://www.reviewjournal.com/sports/dos-santos-fight-miocic-ufc-173|title=Dos Santos to fight Miocic at UFC 173|publisher=reviewjournal.com.com|author=Adam Hill|date=2014-03-03|access-date=2014-03-03}} However, that bout was moved to [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3#The Ultimate Fighter Brazil 3 Finale: dos Santos vs. Miocic|The Ultimate Fighter Brazil 3 Finale]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/chael-sonnen-vs-wanderlei-silva-shifts-to-ufc-175-in-las-vegas-dos-santos-vs-miocic-headlines-in-brazil/|title=Chael Sonnen vs. Wanderlei Silva shifts to UFC 175, dos Santos vs. Miocic headlines in Brazil|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=mmajunkie staff|access-date=2014-04-08|date=2014-04-07}} Subsequently, the bout between [[Dan Henderson]] and [[Daniel Cormier]], briefly linked to [[UFC 175]]{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2014/3/31/5568358/daniel-cormier-vs-dan-henderson-targeted-for-ufc-175|title=Daniel Cormier vs. Dan Henderson targeted for UFC 175|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|access-date=2014-03-31|date=2014-03-31}} was moved up to this event to bolster the card.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/dan-henderson-vs-daniel-cormier-booked-as-ufc-173-co-headliner/|title=Dan Henderson vs. Daniel Cormier booked as UFC 173 co-headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-04-23|date=2014-04-23|archive-date=2017-07-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170708135209/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/dan-henderson-vs-daniel-cormier-booked-as-ufc-173-co-headliner|url-status=dead}}\n\nThe coaches bout between ''[[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3]]'' participants [[Chael Sonnen]] and [[Wanderlei Silva]] was briefly linked to this event. However, the fight was moved twice – first being on May 31, 2014, at [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3#UFC Fight Night: Sonnen vs. Wanderlei|The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3 Finale]],{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2014/2/13/5408210/chael-sonnen-vs-wanderlei-silva-will-headline-fox-sports-1-card-on|title=Chael Sonnen vs. Wanderlei Silva will headline FOX Sports 1 card on May 31|author=Ariel Helwani|publisher=mmafighting.com|access-date=2014-02-13|date=2014-02-13}} then it was to be contested on July 5, 2014, at [[UFC 175]] but the fight eventually was called off for various reasons.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/chael-sonnen-vs-wanderlei-silva-shifts-to-ufc-175-in-las-vegas-dos-santos-vs-miocic-headlines-in-brazil/|title=Chael Sonnen vs. Wanderlei Silva shifts to UFC 175|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=mmajunkie staff|access-date=2014-04-08|date=2014-04-07}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Yves Edwards]] and [[Piotr Hallmann]] was expected to take place at this event. However, the fight was moved to [[UFC Fight Night 42]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/yves-edwards-vs-piotr-hallmann-pushed-back-from-ufc-173-to-ufc-fight-night-43/|title=Yves Edwards vs. Piotr Hallmann pushed back from UFC 173 to UFC Fight Night 42|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2014-04-01|access-date=2014-04-01|author=Staff|archive-date=2014-04-03|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140403214157/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/yves-edwards-vs-piotr-hallmann-pushed-back-from-ufc-173-to-ufc-fight-night-43/|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Kyung Ho Kang]] was expected to face [[Chris Holdsworth]] at the event. However, Kang pulled out of the bout and was replaced by [[Chico Camus]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/chico-camus-in-for-kyung-ho-kang-meets-chris-holdsworth-at-ufc-173/|title=Chico Camus in for Kyung Ho Kang, meets Chris Holdsworth at UFC 173|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=2014-04-05|access-date=2014-04-05|author=Matt Erickson|archive-date=2014-04-09|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140409003203/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/chico-camus-in-for-kyung-ho-kang-meets-chris-holdsworth-at-ufc-173/|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Doo Ho Choi]] was expected to make his promotional debut at the event against [[Sam Sicilia]]. However, Choi was forced out of the bout with an injury,{{cite web|url=http://fightsportasia.com/2014/04/20/doo-ho-choi-injured-out-of-next-months-featherweight-clash-with-sam-sicilia-at-ufc-173/|title=Doo Ho Choi injured, out of next months Featherweight clash with Sam Sicilia at UFC 173|publisher=fightsportasia.com|author=B Wylie|date=2014-04-20|access-date=2014-04-24|archive-date=2014-04-23|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140423103242/http://fightsportasia.com/2014/04/20/doo-ho-choi-injured-out-of-next-months-featherweight-clash-with-sam-sicilia-at-ufc-173/|url-status=dead}} and was replaced by UFC newcomer Aaron Phillips.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2014/5/4/5680644/ufc-173-signs-aaron-phillips-vs-sam-sicilia-doo-ho-choi-injury-mma-news|title=Welcome to the UFC Aaron Phillips|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Zane Simon|date=2014-05-03|access-date=2014-05-03|archive-date=2014-05-05|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140505054419/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2014/5/4/5680644/ufc-173-signs-aaron-phillips-vs-sam-sicilia-doo-ho-choi-injury-mma-news|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Danny Mitchell (fighter)|Danny Mitchell]] was expected to face promotional newcomer [[Li Jingliang]]. However, Mitchell pulled out of the bout with an injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/05/danny-mitchell-out-at-ufc-173-promotion-seeks-welterweight-replacement-against-jingliang-li|title=Danny Mitchell out at UFC 173, promotion seeks welterweight for Jingliang Li|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2014-05-01|access-date=2014-05-01}} Replacing Mitchell was [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Carwin vs. Team Nelson]] cast member [[David Michaud]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/05/tuf-16-vet-david-michaud-signed-to-ufc-meets-jingliang-li-at-ufc-173|title='TUF 16' vet David Michaud signed to UFC, meets Jingliang Li at UFC 173|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2014-05-01|access-date=2014-05-01}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[T.J. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2014/5/27/5754928/ufc-173-salaries-t-j-dillashaw-earns-36000-renan-barao-daniel-cormier|title=UFC 173 salaries: T.J. Dillashaw:''' $36,000 (includes $18,000 win bonus) def. Renan Barão: $74,000\n* '''Daniel Cormier:''' $170,000 (includes $85,000 win bonus) def. Dan Henderson: $100,000\n* '''Robbie Lawler:''' $200,000 (includes $100,000 win bonus) def. Jake Ellenberger: $68,000\n* '''Takeya Mizugaki:''' $58,000 (includes $29,000 win bonus) def. Francisco Rivera: $15,000\n* '''James Krause:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Jamie Varner: $17,000\n* '''Michael Chiesa:''' $40,000 (includes $20,000 win bonus) def. Francisco Trinaldo: $12,000\n* '''Tony Ferguson:''' $40,000 (includes $20,000 win bonus) def. Katsunori Kikuno: $10,000\n* '''Chris Holdsworth:''' $30,000 (includes $15,000 win bonus) def. Chico Camus: $12,000\n* '''Mitch Clarke:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Al Iaquinta: $14,000\n* '''Vinc Pichel:''' $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def. Anthony Njokuani: $20,000\n* '''Sam Sicilia:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Aaron Phillips: $8,000\n* '''Li Jingliang:''' $16,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. The DVD was distributed by the label ''[[Anchor Bay Entertainment]]''.\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Jon Jones]] and [[Glover Teixeira]], briefly linked as the event headliner at [[UFC 169]],{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jon-jones-glover-teixeira-mir-overeem-top-ufc-169-on-super-bowl-weekend|title=Don Johnson-Glover Teixeira, Mir-Overeem top UFC 169 on Super Bowl weekend|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=3 October 2013|access-date=October 3, 2013|archive-date=3 October 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131003234646/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jon-jones-glover-teixeira-mir-overeem-top-ufc-169-on-super-bowl-weekend|url-status=dead}} [[UFC 170]]{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/11/champ-jon-jones-vs-glover-teixeira-headlines-februarys-ufc-170-event-in-las-vegas/|title=Champ Jon Jones vs. Glover Teixeira headlines February's UFC 170|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2013-11-13}} and [[UFC 171]]{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/12/jon-jones-vs-glover-teixeira-headlines-ufc-171-in-dallas/|title=Jon Jones vs. Glover Teixeira headlines UFC 171 in Dallas|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=December 4, 2013}}{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/12/jon-jones-vs-glover-teixeira-title-fight-moved-to-ufc-172/|title=Jon Jones vs. Glover Teixeira title fight moved to UFC 172|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|date=13 December 2013 |access-date=2013-12-13}} served as the main event.\n\n[[Danny Castillo]] was originally expected to face [[Isaac Vallie-Flagg]] at this event. However, Castillo was replaced by [[Takanori Gomi]] and instead fought [[Charlie Brenneman]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/The-Fireball-Kid-Returns-in-April|title=The Fireball Kid Returns in April|publisher=UFC.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2014-03-04|date=2014-03-04}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/danny-castillo-rebooked-against-charlie-brenneman-at-ufc-172/|title=Danny Castillo rebooked against Charlie Brenneman at UFC 172|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-03-05|date=2014-03-05|archive-date=2014-03-06|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140306031958/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/danny-castillo-rebooked-against-charlie-brenneman-at-ufc-172/|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Tarec Saffiedine]] was originally scheduled to face [[Jake Ellenberger]] in a welterweight bout, but withdrew due to a back injury. As a result, Ellenberger was removed from the card and was rescheduled to face [[Robbie Lawler]] at [[UFC 173]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/with-saffiedine-injured-ellenberger-vs-lawler-booked-for-ufc-173/|title=With Saffiedine injured, Ellenberger vs. Lawler booked for UFC 173 |publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|access-date=2014-03-28|date=2014-03-28}}\n\nJust one week before the event, [[Bobby Green]] suffered an injury and was forced off the card.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/bobby-green-out-of-ufc-172-vs-jim-miller-ufc-seeking-replacement|title=Bobby Green out of UFC 172 vs. Jim Miller, UFC seeking replacement|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=April 19, 2014}} [[Yancy Medeiros]], who was supposed to fight Joe Ellenberger, stepped in and faced Green's original opponent [[Jim Miller (fighter)|Jim Miller]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/source-yancy-meideiros-steps-in-to-fight-jim-miller-at-ufc-172|title=Source: Yancy Meideiros steps in to fight Jim Miller at UFC 172|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=April 19, 2014}} Ellenberger was expected to face returning veteran [[Vagner Rocha]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2014/4/20/5633572/vagner-rocha-vs-joe-ellenberger-being-targeted-for-ufc-172|title=Vagner Rocha vs. Joe Ellenberger being targeted for UFC 172|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=MMA Fighting Newswire|date=April 20, 2014}} Subsequently, just days after accepting the bout, Rocha injured himself in the week leading up to the event. With no time to find a suitable replacement, Ellenberger was pulled from the card as well.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/vagner-rocha-hurt-ufc-172-bout-with-joe-ellenberger-scrapped/|title=Vagner Rocha hurt, UFC 172 bout with Joe Ellenberger scrapped|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=April 23, 2014|archive-date=April 24, 2014|access-date=April 24, 2014|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140424050857/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/vagner-rocha-hurt-ufc-172-bout-with-joe-ellenberger-scrapped/|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent event was released on DVD on June 17, 2014. The DVD was distributed by ''[[Anchor Bay Entertainment]]''.\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Jon Jones]] and [[Glover Teixeira]], briefly linked as the event headliner at [[UFC 169]],{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jon-jones-glover-teixeira-mir-overeem-top-ufc-169-on-super-bowl-weekend|title=Jon Jones-Glover Teixeira, Mir-Overeem top UFC 169 on Super Bowl weekend|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=3 October 2013|access-date=October 3, 2013|archive-date=3 October 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131003234646/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jon-jones-glover-teixeira-mir-overeem-top-ufc-169-on-super-bowl-weekend|url-status=dead}} and [[UFC 170]]{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/11/champ-jon-jones-vs-glover-teixeira-headlines-februarys-ufc-170-event-in-las-vegas/|title=Champ Jon Jones vs. Glover Teixeira headlines February's UFC 170|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2013-11-13}} was expected to headline this event.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/12/jon-jones-vs-glover-teixeira-headlines-ufc-171-in-dallas/|title=Jon Jones vs. Glover Teixeira headlines UFC 171 in Dallas|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=December 4, 2013}} However, the fight was postponed and moved to [[UFC 172]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/12/jon-jones-vs-glover-teixeira-title-fight-moved-to-ufc-172/|title=Jon Jones vs. Glover Teixeira title fight moved to UFC 172|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=13 December 2013 |access-date=2013-12-13}}\n\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] bout between [[Johny Hendricks]] and [[Robbie Lawler]] for the vacant title headlined the event.\n\n[[Julianna Peña]] was expected to face [[Jéssica Andrade]] at this event. However, Pena pulled out of the bout citing an injury to the knee and was replaced by [[Raquel Pennington]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/01/raquel-pennington-replaces-injured-julianna-pena-at-ufc-171/|title=Raquel Pennington replaces injured Julianna Pena at UFC 171|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2014-01-30|access-date=2014-01-30}}\n\n[[Darrell Montague]] was expected to face [[Will Campuzano]] at this event. However, Montague was forced to pull out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by [[Justin Scoggins]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/01/justin-scoggins-replaces-darrell-montague-at-ufc-171-in-dallas/|title=Justin Scoggins replaces Darrell Montague at UFC 171 in Dallas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-01-28|date=2014-01-28|archive-date=2017-08-26|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170826113317/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/01/justin-scoggins-replaces-darrell-montague-at-ufc-171-in-dallas|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Thiago Silva (fighter)|Thiago Silva]] was expected to face [[Ovince St. Preux]] at this event. However, Silva was removed from the bout due to police apprehension and release{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2014/3/7/5482418/thiago-silva-to-be-released-from-police-custody-friday|title=Thiago Silva to be released from jail Friday |publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-03-12|date=2014-03-12}} for multiple counts of aggravated assault. This led to his subsequent release from the promotion.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/ufcs-thiago-silva-jailed-in-florida-on-four-charges-including-attempted-murder/|title=UFC's Thiago Silva jailed in Florida on four charges, including multiple counts of aggravated assault=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-02-07|date=2014-02-07|archive-date=2014-02-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140207143935/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/ufcs-thiago-silva-jailed-in-florida-on-four-charges-including-attempted-murder/|url-status=dead}} He was replaced by [[Nikita Krylov]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/nikita-krylov-drops-to-205-replaces-thiago-silva-at-ufc-171/|title=Nikita Krylov drops to 205, replaces Thiago Silva at UFC 171|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|access-date=2014-02-07|date=2014-02-07|archive-date=2014-02-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140208054139/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/nikita-krylov-drops-to-205-replaces-thiago-silva-at-ufc-171/|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Tor Troéng]] was expected to face [[Bubba McDaniel]] at this event. However, Troeng was forced out of the bout due to an injury and was replaced by UFC newcomer [[Sean Strickland]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/sean-strickland-replaces-tor-troeng-faces-robert-mcdaniel-at-ufc-171/|title=Sean Strickland replaces Tor Troeng, faces Robert McDaniel at UFC 171|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-03-03|date=2014-03-03}}\n\nThe televised preliminary portion of the card aired on [[Fox Sports 2]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/reminder-saturdays-ufc-171-prelims-move-to-fox-sports-2|title=Saturday's UFC 171 prelims move to FOX Sports 2|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-03-10|date=2014-03-10}}\n\n[[Renée Forte]] weighed in one pound over the lightweight limit of 156 lb. [[mixed martial arts]] event held on November 9, 2013, at the [[Goiânia Arena]] in [[Goiânia, Brazil]].\n*{{cite web|url=https://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2013/08/anuncio-de-barueri-sp-como-sede-do-ufc-do-dia-9-de-outubro-fica-proximo.html|title=Anúncio de Barueri-SP como sede do UFC do dia 9 de Henderson Headlines UFC Goiania|publisher=ufc.com|date=2013-09-05|access-date=2013-09-05}} The event was broadcast live on [[Fox Sports 1]].\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a rematch between longtime multi-divisional contenders [[Vitor Belfort]] and [[Dan Henderson]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/belfort-vs-henderson-2-set-for-205-pounds-official-for-ufc-fight-night-32|title=Belfort vs. Henderson 2 set for 205 pounds, official for UFC Fight Night Henderson defeated Belfort in their first encounter in 2006 at [[Pride 32]] in a unanimous decision.\n\nThe co-main event featured the long-awaited bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil]] competitors [[Cezar Ferreira]] and [[Daniel Sarafian]]. Both men were originally scheduled to face each other in the finale to determine the winner of the inaugural TUF series for [[Brazil]]. However, Sarafian injured himself before the finale in June 2012 and was forced out of the finals.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/06/with-mutante-vs-sarafian-official-ufc-147s-main-card-complete-with-five-bouts|title=Sergio Moraes replaces injured Daniel Sarafian in 'TUF: Brazil' middleweight final|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2012-06-18|access-date=2013-11-08|archive-date=2013-11-09|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131109212045/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2012/06/with-mutante-vs-sarafian-official-ufc-147s-main-card-complete-with-five-bouts|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Thiago Tavares]] was expected to face [[Quinn Mulhern]] at the event. However, Mulhern pulled out of the bout citing an injury and was replaced by [[Justin Salas]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/justin-salas-replaces-injured-quinn-mulhern-at-ufc-fight-night-32|title=Justin Salas replaces injured Quinn Mulhern at UFC Fight Night 32|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-09-27|access-date=2013-09-27|archive-date=2013-09-27|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130927182742/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/justin-salas-replaces-injured-quinn-mulhern-at-ufc-fight-night-32|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Johnny Eduardo]] and [[Lucas Martins]] were originally scheduled to face each other in a bantamweight bout on this card. However, both fighters were injured and the fight was removed from the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/johnny-eduardo-vs-lucas-martins-scratched-from-ufc-fight-night-32|title=Johnny Eduardo vs. Lucas Martins scratched from UFC Fight Night Kentucky]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/ufc-fight-for-the-troops-3-event-targeted-for-nov-6|title=UFC Fight for the Troops 3 event targeted for Nov. 6|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-07-06|access-date=2013-07-06|archive-date=2016-03-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160304082630/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/ufc-fight-for-the-troops-3-event-targeted-for-nov-6|url-status=dead}}{{cite The event was broadcast live on [[Fox Sports 1]].\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the fourth that [[Ultimate Fighting Championship|UFC]] hosted in cooperation with a US military base, following [[UFC Fight Night: Sanchez vs. Riggs]] in 2006, [[UFC: Fight for the Troops]] in 2008 and [[UFC: Fight for the Troops 2]] in 2011. Featured on the card were several [[United States Armed Forces]] military veterans including [[Tim Kennedy (fighter)|Tim Kennedy]], [[Liz Carmouche]], [[Colton Smith (fighter)|Colton Smith]] and [[Neil Magny]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Military-Vets-Well-Represented-on-Fight-for-The-Troops-Card|title=Military vets well represented on Fight for the Troops card|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2013-10-30|access-date=2013-10-30}}\n\nA bout between [[George Roop]] and [[Francisco Rivera (fighter)|Francisco Rivera]], previously linked to [[UFC 166]] was moved to this event to bolster the card.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/ufc-166-adds-eye-vs-kaufman-sends-rivera-vs-roop-to-ufc-fight-night-31|title=Rivera vs. Roop to UFC Fight Night 31|publisher=MMAJunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-08-23|access-date=2013-08-23|archive-date=2013-08-24|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130824000820/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/ufc-166-adds-eye-vs-kaufman-sends-rivera-vs-roop-to-ufc-fight-night-31|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Nik Lentz]] was briefly scheduled to face [[Dennis Bermudez]] at the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.fighters.com/08/23/nik-lentz-vs-dennis-bermudez-signed-for-ufn-31|title=Nik Lentz Vs Dennis Bermudez Signed For UFN 31|publisher=fighters.com|author=John Petit|date=2013-08-23|access-date=2013-08-23|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131005120948/http://www.fighters.com/08/23/nik-lentz-vs-dennis-bermudez-signed-for-ufn-31|archive-date=2013-10-05}} However, Lentz was pulled from the pairing with Bermudez in favor of a bout with [[Chad Mendes]] on December 14, 2013, at [[UFC on Fox 9]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/9/17/4741654/chad-mendes-vs-nik-lentz-booked-for-ufc-on-fox-9|title=Chad Mendes vs. Nik Lentz booked for UFC on FOX 9 |publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2013-09-17|access-date=2013-09-17}} Bermudez then faced [[Steven Siler]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/dennis-bermudez-vs-steven-siler-joins-ufc-fight-night-31-troops-lineup|title=Dennis Bermudez vs. Steven Siler joins UFC Fight Night 31 'Troops' of their bout with an injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/michael-bisping-out-at-ufc-fight-night-30-lyoto-machida-now-faces-mark-munoz|title=Michael Bisping out at UFC Fight Kennedy instead faced [[Rafael Natal]] in the event headliner.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/tim-kennedy-gets-rafael-natal-at-ufcs-fight-for-the-troops-3|title=Tim Kennedy gets description|UFC mixed martial arts television series and event in 2013}}\n{{Infobox television\n|image = \n|genre = [[reality television|Reality]], [[Broadcasting of sports events|Sports]]\n|runtime = 60 minutes\n|creator = [[Frank Fertitta III]], [[Lorenzo Fertitta]], [[Dana White]]\n|starring = Dana White, [[Ronda Rousey]], and [[Miesha Tate]]\n|country = United States\n|network = [[Fox Sports 1]]\n|first_aired = {{Start date|2013|09|04}}\n|last_aired = {{End date|2013|11|27}}\n}}\n\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate''''' (also known as '''''The Ultimate Fighter 18''''') is the eighteenth U.S.-based installment of the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC)-produced [[reality television]] series ''[[The Ultimate Fighter]]''.\n\nThe series was officially announced by the UFC in March 2013 with the revelation that for the first time women will serve as coaches and the contestants will be both male and female.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/03/tuf-18-features-men-and-women-rousey-and-tate-zingano-winner-serve-as-coaches |title='TUF 18' features men and women, Rousey and Tate-Zingano winner serve as coaches |publisher=MMAJunkie.com |date=March 16, 2013 |access-date=August 23, 2013}}{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/08/ultimate-fighter-season-18-cast-revealed-including-larosa-baszler-modafferi |title='Ultimate Fighter' Season 18 cast revealed, including LaRosa, Baszler, Modafferi |publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Staff |date=2013-08-15|access-date=2013-08-23}} Women's Bantamweight Champion [[Ronda Rousey]] was selected to coach against the winner of the [[Miesha Tate]] and [[Cat Zingano]] bout on [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen Finale#The Ultimate Fighter 17 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter 17 finale]] in April 2013. Zingano won the fight, but a few weeks prior to filming was removed from the coaching position due to an injury and replaced by Tate.{{cite web|url=http://msn.foxsports.com/ufc/story/miesha-tate-replacing-cat-zingano-the-ultimate-fighter-ronda-rousey-fox-sports-1-052813|title=Tate replacing Zingano on TUF|publisher=FoxSports.com|date=2013-05-28|access-date=2013-05-28|author=A.J. Perez}}\n\nThe season was the first to air on [[Fox Sports 1]]{{cite web |url=http://www.mixedmartialarts.com/mma.cfm?go=news.detail&gid=436711 |title=TUF 18 starts Wed 9/4 at 10 PM on Fox Sports 1 |publisher=mixedmartialarts.com |date=April 15, 2013 |access-date=August 23, 2013}} and debuted on September 4, 2013, and also the first time that it had been seen on [[BT Sport]] in the UK debuting on September 5, 2013, at 11PM also.{{cite web |url=https://www.telegraph.co.uk/sport/othersports/ufc/10053247/New-UFC-deal-with-BT-Sport-goes-beyond-just-fighting-into-a-culture-shift-says-Garry-Cook.html |title=TUF 18 starts in UK on BT Sport|publisher=telegraph.co.uk |date=May 13, 2013}}\n\n==Cast==\n\n===Coaches===\n{{Col-begin}}\n{{Col-break}}\n;{{color box|#00FF00|border=silver}} {{color box|black|border=silver}} Team coach\n{{Col-break}}\n;{{color box|blue|border=silver}} {{color box|yellow|border=silver}} Team Tate:\n*[[Miesha Tate]], head coach\n*Thonglor \"Master Thong\" Armatsena, Muay Thai coach\n*Ricky coach\n{{col-end}}\n\n===Fighters===\n*'''Team Rousey'''\n**'''Female fighters:''' [[Shayna Baszler]], [[Jessamyn Duke]], [[Peggy Morgan]], [[Jessica Rakoczy]]\n**'''Male Fighters:''' [[Chris Beal]], [[Davey Grant]], Anthony Gutierrez, Michael Wootten\n*'''Team Tate'''\n**'''Female fighters:''' [[Julianna Peña]], [[Sarah Moras]], [[Raquel Pennington]], [[Roxanne Modafferi]]\n**'''Male Fighters:''' Cody Bollinger, [[Chris Holdsworth]], [[Josh Hill (fighter)|Josh Hill]], Tim Gorman (*Louis Fisette)\n\n''* Gorman got injured and was replaced on episode 2 by Fisette''\n\n; Fighters eliminated during the entry round:\n*Laura Howarth, [[Danny Martinez]], Revelina Berto, Emil Hartsner, Bethany Marshall, [[Valérie Létourneau]], Lee Sandmeier, [[Tonya Evinger]], Sirwan Kakai, [[Paddy Holohan]], [[Colleen Schneider]], [[Gina Mazany]], Matt Munsey, [[Tara LaRosa]], Rafael De Freitas{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/09/episode-no-1-recap-the-ultimate-fighter-18-team-rousey-vs-team-tate |title=Episode No. 1 recap: 'The Ultimate Fighter 18: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate' |publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=Staff |date=2013-09-05|access-date=2013-09-05}}\n\n==Episodes==\n\n;'''Episode 1: \"History in the Making\" (September 4, 2013)'''\n*Ronda Rousey enters the Ultimate Fighter training center; she is surprised to see Miesha Tate enter the center and state she is there to coach.\n*Dana White explains that [[Cat Zingano]] injured her knee and that Tate stepped up to be her replacement; Rousey is relieved, explaining she thought she was being replaced.\n*Unlike previous seasons, this season will feature women for the first time alongside men, all in the 135 pound divisions.\n*White greets the 32 fighters (16 men and 16 women) and implores them to leave it all in the cage the next day.\n*The 16 preliminary fights began:\n\n:'''Jessamyn Duke''' defeated Laura Howarth via submission (triangle choke) at 3:50 in the first round.\n:'''Davey Grant''' defeated Danny Martinez via submission (armbar) at 2:02 in the second round.\n:'''Jessica Rakoczy''' defeated Revelina Berto via submission (omoplata) at 2:32 in the first round.\n:'''Michael Wootten''' defeated Emil Hartsner via unanimous decision after three rounds.\n:'''Peggy Morgan''' defeated Bethany Marshall via TKO (punches) at 2:58 in the first round.\n:'''Roxanne Modafferi''' defeated Valerie Letourneau via submission (rear naked choke) at 3:36 in the first round.\n:'''Tim Gorman''' defeated Lee Sandmeier via TKO (punches) at 4:46 in the first round.\n:'''Raquel Pennington''' defeated Tonya Evinger via submission (guillotine choke) in the second round.\n:'''Chris Beal''' defeated Sirwan Kakai via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:'''Josh Hill''' defeated Patrick Holohan via majority decision after two rounds.\n:'''Shayna Baszler''' defeated Colleen Schneider via submission (armbar) at 4:24 in the first round.\n:'''Chris Holdsworth''' defeated Louis Fisette via submission (arm triangle choke) at 4:09 in the first round.\n:'''Julianna Peña''' defeated Gina Mazany via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:'''Anthony Gutierrez''' defeated Matt Munsey via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:'''Sarah Moras''' defeated Tara LaRosa via unanimous decision after two rounds.\n:'''Cody Bollinger''' defeated Rafael de Freitas via TKO (strikes) at 1:54 in the second round.\n\n*White greeted the winners and announced that fighters could receive $25,000 for ''Knockout of the Season'', ''Submission of the Season'' and ''Fight of the Season''.\n*Rousey and Tate then picked teams and White flipped a coin (green for Rousey, blue for Tate). Rousey won the coin toss and opted to choose the first fight.\n*'''Women's selection:'''\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n|-\n!Coach\n!1st Pick\n!2nd Pick\n!3rd Pick\n!4th Pick\n|-\n!Tate\n|Julianna Peña\n|Sarah Moras\n|Raquel Pennington\n|Roxanne Modafferi\n|-\n!Rousey\n|Shayna Baszler\n|Jessamyn Duke\n|Peggy Morgan\n|Jessica Rakoczy\n|}\n\n*'''Men's selection:'''\n{| class=\"wikitable\"\n|-\n!Coach\n!1st Pick\n!2nd Pick\n!3rd Pick\n!4th Pick\n|-\n!Tate\n|Cody Bollinger\n|Chris Holdsworth\n|Josh Hill\n|Tim Gorman\n|-\n!Rousey\n|Chris Beal\n|Davey Grant\n|Anthony Gutierrez\n|Michael Wootten\n|}\n*White then put Rousey on the spot, forcing her to choose the first fight right there. She chose to match Baszler against Peña, the two first female picks from both teams.\n\n;'''Episode 2: \"Ladies First\" (September 11, 2013)'''\n\n*Team Tate's Chris Holdsworth admires the situation and debates the possibility of sexual relationships as the season progresses.\n*The women continue to state how seriously they are taking the competition and nothing will interfere with that.\n*White arrives at the gym and is accompanied by a doctor who holds the results of Tim Gorman's MRI test. It is bad news, as the doctor reveals a tear to his hamstring. Gorman is devastated by the news and White pulls him from the competition.\n*Gorman's replacement on Team Tate is Louis Fisette, who was submitted by Holdsworth in the elimination round after an intense battle.\n*'''Julianna Peña''' defeated Shayna Baszler via submission (rear naked choke) at 3:08 in the second round.\n*After Rousey went after Tate's top female pick in week one, Tate decides to flip the script by selecting the champion's top pick. The match will see Team Tate's second pick Holdsworth go up against Team Rousey's top selection Chris Beal, who earlier in the episode had complained about an injured hand.\n*The episode closes with both teams leaving the gym and as Rousey walks by Tate, she angrily accuses her of smiling at Baszler's pain.\n\n;'''Episode 3: \"Stick and Move\" (September 18, 2013)'''\n*Tate visits the house to bring Julianna Peña a victory butter-fat milkshake and also consoles her friend Shayna Baszler, saying sorry she has to be on Rousey's team. But Baszler says Rousey has \"got her back\" after her loss.\n*Cody Bollinger states that someone told the green team about Team Tate's women's match-ups and he blames Julianna Peña. He then confronts Peña in the van ride back to the house accusing her of the deed. Feeling like she is being attacked, Peña then points the finger at Roxanne Modafferi because she sleeps in the same room as Team Rousey, but her teammates back her up. Later, Peña apologizes to Modafferi who forgives her.\n*Jessamyn Duke jokes around with Raquel Pennington telling her that she got access to the \"secret list\" of women's matches. Duke says that Bollinger overheard and used this as an excuse to blame Peña, just because he is irritated by her actions in the house.\n*Both teams gets a private suite at ''[[Red Rock Casino]]'' to watch the latest UFC fight. While there, Rousey and Tate get into a verbal altercation at the bar when Tate feels Rousey is standing too close to her boyfriend/trainer Bryan Caraway. Rousey then rehashes what Caraway tweeted in March 2012 how he wanted to knock her teeth down her throat on ''Twitter'' which he had previously apologized for.\n*Tate retaliates by criticizing Rousey's stand-up game by saying she does not know how to \"hit the pads,\" which her trainer Edmond Tarverdyan does not take well and goes on to criticize Tate and Caraway.\n*'''Chris Holdsworth''' defeated Chris Beal via submission (guillotine) at 4:16 in the first round.\n*After the fight, the next match is chosen by Tate: Roxanne Modafferi vs. Jessica Rakoczy.\n\n;'''Episode 4: \"Use the Force\" (September 25, 2013)'''\n*Former UFC fighter [[Dennis Hallman]] joins the blue team as a guest coach and gets a chilly reception by Team Rousey's trainer Edmond Tarverdyan who \"eyeballs\" him up and down. On camera, Tarverdyan challenges Hallman to a fight. Off camera, the Armenian is tempted by Hallman to engage in combat for strange reasons. Tarverdyan claims he is being targeted to give producers a reason to kick him off the show. Rousey then confronts Hallman and tells him if he wants to \"look like a fucking man,\" he will fight Tarverdyan when there are no cameras around.\n*The Caraway/Rousey Twitter incident is brought up again by Team Rousey and the drama ensues, making White play the mediator between both teams, saying that it is not good for the sport.\n*During a light sparring session with Modafferi, Raquel Pennington wants to go harder, so Tate starts having the women train with the men. Sarah Moras wants to be one of the guys and asks them to hit her and not hold back, so Cody Bollinger and Louis Fisette oblige.\n*'''Jessica Rakoczy''' defeated Roxanne Modafferi via TKO (punches) at 2:33 in the second round.\n*Modafferi is very upset and Rakoczy consoles her, stating that things like this are what make a champion.\n*Both teams feel bad for Modafferi's loss because of how hard she worked and nice she is, especially her friend Shayna Baszler who also suffered an emotional breakdown after her loss.\n*After the fight, Rousey is back in control and chooses the next match: Davey Grant vs. Louis Fisette.\n\n;'''Episode 5: \"Redemption\" (October 2, 2013)'''\n*Raquel Pennington expresses to her teammates what it is like being a homosexual athlete.\n*During a game of \"truth or dare,\" Louis Fisette gets offended when Anthony Gutierrez answers his truth question by saying that the Canadian is the weakest link of the males on the show.\n*Back at the house, some fighters on both teams struggle with the pressure to maintain weight before their fights by not eating \"clean.\"\n*Rousey's mother, former judo champion [[AnnMaria De Mars]], stops by the gym to inspire her daughter's team.\n*To ease tension between the teams, Tate and her trainers play a joke on Team Rousey's trainer Edmond Tarverdyan by placing a figurine of ''[[Sesame Street]]''{{'}}s \"[[Count von Count|the Count]]\" who they think has a resemblance to, complete with an eyebrow waxing coupon attached it for his unibrow. But thinking he will do something bad in retaliation, Rousey hides it from him.\n*'''Davey Grant''' defeated Louis Fisette via submission (rear naked choke) at 1:01 in the second round.\n*Team Rousey evens up the score and matches Jessamyn Duke against Pennington.\n\n;'''Episode 6: \"Little Princesses\" (October 9, 2013)'''\n*Julianna Peña gives Pennington and Sarah Moras makeovers which she calls her \"little princesses.\"\n*The fighters take a break by going on a roadtrip to [[Green Valley Ranch]] where the ''[[Hooters]]'' girls set up private catering poolside.\n*Duke decides to stay at the house to shadowbox and focus on her fight.\n*Anthony Gutierrez annoys the female fighters in the house with his personality and crazy antics while they are sleeping.\n*Tate brings in two time [[Muay Thai]] world champion and Hall of Famer, [[Melchor Menor]], to coach Pennington with her [[Muay thai clinch|anti-clinch]] game.\n*'''Raquel Pennington''' defeated Jessamyn Duke via unanimous decision in three rounds.\n*After a strong first round by Duke, Pennington shut down Duke's forward kicking and closed distance for more effective striking.\n*Rousey, Tate, White, and all the fighters expressed how intense and close the fight was as well as deep respect for both Pennington and Duke as fighters. This ended up being the fight of the season.\n*Having regained matchmaking control, Tate announces the next match: Josh Hill vs. Michael Wootten.\n\n;'''Episode 7: \"Zone In\" (October 16, 2013)'''\n*Rousey visits the fighter house to deliver gifts for all the fighting fathers on Father's Day.\n*Team Tate plays another prank on Edmond Tarverdyan when they put pictures of [[Missi Pyle]] as Fran from [[DodgeBall: A True Underdog Story|Dodgeball]] entitled, \"Edmond Rousey\" up all over the gym, grabbing White's attention. White then takes it upon himself to take down all the pictures so another war between the teams does not ignite again, but fails to remove all the pictures.\n*Rousey finds out and goes ballistic, calling the prank and Tate and her trainers racists due to the nature of facial stereotypes of Armenians.\n*'''Michael Wootten''' defeated Josh Hill via unanimous decision in three rounds.\n*Tate goes over to Rousey and her trainers to shake hands and once again, they flip the bird. Rousey says that Tate is acting like a \"fake bitch\" by insulting her behind her back and then being nice to her face. Rousey then calls it a battle of \"fake nice vs. real mean.\"\n*Rousey is back in control and picks the last two preliminary match-ups: Peggy Morgan vs. Sarah Moras, and then Anthony Gutierrez vs. Cody Bollinger.\n\n;'''Episode 8: \"Tied\" (October 23, 2013)'''\n*White, Rousey, Tate and the fighters recap the season's highs and lows so far.\n*Interviews with the coaches and fighters are shown.\n*Match highlights from the previous fights are also shown.\n*Rousey and Tate's disputes throughout the season are discussed, including Rousey's constant flipping of the bird.\n\n;'''Episode 9: \"Sacrifice\" (October 30, 2013)'''\n*Team Tate's first pick Cody Bollinger mentally breaks down and decides to quit after not making weight before his fight.\n*White calls both teams out to the floor for Bollinger's announcement and expels him from the show directly after.\n*The coaches are called in to White's office to discuss what happened with Bollinger. Tate explains she tried to help him, causing Rousey to remark that if it was one of her fighters, she would take the blame.\n*The fighting commission decides to either forfeit the match or have Anthony Gutierrez fight Chris Beal, who was selected as the wildcard.\n*Gutierrez decides not to cut weight again for another fight and goes the easier route with forfeit to go into the semifinals.\n*'''Sarah Moras''' defeated Peggy Morgan via submission (armbar) at 4:39 in the first round.\n*To White's surprise, both coaches agree on the men's and women's semifinal match-ups: Chris Holdsworth vs. Michael Wootten; Julianna Peña against Moras; Davey Grant against Gutierrez; Raquel Pennington vs. Jessica Rakoczy.\n\n;'''Episode 10: \"HWPO\" (November 6, 2013)'''\n*Rousey stops by the fighter house to bring her team's fighters some fast food, and also mixes drinks and shares shots with them.\n*Meanwhile, Sarah Moras discusses with her fellow Team Tate semifinalist Raquel Pennington that she feels like she is the underdog and Julianna Peña, who trains with Tate, is their team favorite, making her feel left out of her much-needed personal training, as the rest of the team believe Holdsworth is their \"golden boy\" favorite.\n*Holdsworth and Wootten have different reactions leading up to their fight. Holdsworth alienates himself from the rest of the fighters, only concentrating on his mind and body, while Wootten is struggling mentally and feels fatigued and homesick being away from his family.\n*The semifinalist fighters and coaches take a trip to the local ''[[Harley Davidson]]'' dealership for their \"motorcycle boot camp\" by testing out their potential rides and also go shopping for new gear.\n*A surprise guest rider shows up and it turns out to be UFC heavyweight champion, [[Cain Velasquez]], who gives the fighters some words of encouragement.\n*Rousey shows off her strength by lifting a motorcycle as demonstrated by the Harley rep.\n*Tate decides to [[Silly String]] her team to loosen the tension. She also brings in former [[Central Washington University]] wrestler turned motivational speaker Kenny Salvini. He had a severe accident while snowboarding that rendered him quadriplegic and talks to the team about overcoming setbacks in life.\n*TUF Season 5 winner and [[Brazilian jiu jitsu]] blackbelt, [[Nate Diaz]], is invited by Rousey to help her team train their BJJ techniques.\n*'''Chris Holdsworth''' defeated Michael Wootten by submission (rear naked choke) at 3:11 in the first round.\n*Holdworth dedicates his win to his mentor, UFC legend [[Royce Gracie]], and says his motto, H.W.P.O. (\"Hard Work Pays Off\").\n\n;'''Episode 11: \"Mean Girls\" (November 13, 2013)'''\n*The charges of favoritism continue towards Peña and it disrupts Moras' training, making the Canadian feel \"out of the loop,\" and her teammates turn against Peña at the house.\n*Tate put Moras self doubts to rest when she says that she will remove herself from coaching, and Bryan Caraway and the rest of the trainers will get more involved and try to help her by having separate coaches and corners for each fighter.\n*Roommates Raquel Pennington and Peña become agitated towards each other as the close quarters, daily routines and monotony of the house sets in. This causes Peña to call all the female fighters \"[[Mean Girls]],\" after the [[Lindsay Lohan]] film.\n*Rousey stops by the TUF house wanting to personalize the female fighters' ''UFC'' T-shirts by cutting them up to make holes for ventilation which everyone dubs getting your shirt \"Rondafied.\"\n*Tate also visits the house before the fights and has a discussion with Peña. She is honest in saying there may be some favoritism towards Peña, but claims it is because she is the hardest working and most willing-to-learn female fighter on the team.\n*'''Julianna Peña''' defeated Sarah Moras by submission (guillotine choke) at 3:31 in the second round.\n*Tearful and frustrated in losing, Moras apologizes for her lackluster performance to White and thanks him for being on the show.\n\n;'''Episode 12: \"One Punch Away\" (November 20, 2013)'''\n*Rousey and her trainers visit the fighter house to have an Armenian barbecue with TUF season 5 runner-up Manvel \"Manny\" Gamburyan doing the grilling for all the fighters. But with their fight coming up, Davey Grant only has a tiny bite to eat while Anthony Gutierrez, not worrying about making weight, downs his meal asking for seconds.\n*The feud between Rousey and Tate reaches new heights when they are tasked at racing up an indoor rock climbing wall during the Coaches' Challenge. Rousey and Tate were neck and neck until the champion pulled ahead at the end to win her team $10,000 ($1,500 each), and also beating Tate once again in the process, as she screams \"fuck you bitch!\" and flips the bird on the way back down.\n*Gutierrez is constantly scrutinized by his coaches and teammates about his eating habits. He responds by claiming that \"they are jealous and wish they could eat like [him].\"\n*However, the morning of the weigh-ins, Gutierrez weighs 145 lb. He tries an Epsom salt bath, running on the treadmill with a plastic sweatsuit, and the sauna, but can only get down to 140 lb.\n*Gutierrez got a bye into the semifinals when he was scheduled to fight Cody Bollinger who missed weight. Gutierrez would find himself in a similar situation this time around when he was scheduled to face Grant.\n*Gutierrez was unable to make weight and Gamburyan lectured him about how big of a mistake he made, and that the UFC was too high a level for him.\n*White then expelled Gutierrez from the show and gave Grant a direct advance into the finale to fight against Chris Holdsworth.\n*After Gutierrez's expulsion, Rousey felt that it was her fault that he missed weight and that the following day, she will make weight to show her team how to properly make weight.\n*White responded saying that Gutierrez has no heart and that the competition weeds out the weak. He also says Gutierrez should get a regular job and make MMA his hobby.\n*This is the first time in TUF history that two fighters in the same season missed weight.\n\n;'''Episode 13: \"Aiming For the Top\" (November 27, 2013)'''\n*The last semifinals female match between Pennington and Rakoczy does not go off without a hitch, when both fighters get injured during training leading up to the fight.\n*Pennington hurts her right hand grappling Louis Fisette, and Rakoczy injures her shoulder sparring with Chris Beal.\n*'''Jessica Rakoczy''' defeated Raquel Pennington by unanimous decision in three rounds.\n*Rakoczy dominated Pennington with her boxing skills in the last two rounds and defended well against Pennington's takedown attempts, much to everyone's surprise since she was considered one-dimensional.\n*Being that both fighters had pro boxing matches under their belts and it was expected be a slugfest, White expressed that this fight was not what he thought it was going to be and he does not know what happened to Pennington's head after mixing it up with Rakoczy in the first round.\n*Tate could not help but cry with Pennington after her loss.\n*The finalists fighting in the TUF finale at the [[Mandalay Bay]] are; for the men: Chris Holdsworth vs. Davey Grant and for the women: Rakoczy will face Julianna Peña.\n*Since Rousey flipped Tate off the entire season, Tate decides to give the judoka a parting gift of a pair of socks that have the middle finger on it. But when she holds them up in front of Rousey's face during their stare-down, the champion smacks the socks out of Tate's hand and continues the staredown, as her adversary blows her a kiss a few seconds later.\n\n==Tournament Bracket==\n\n===Female Fighters===\n{{Round8\n\n\n||{{color box|#00FF00|border=silver}} 2 Shayna Baszler|2|{{color box|blue|border=silver}} 1 '''Julianna
