'''Sheldon Westcott''' (submission of Vik Grujic)\n\n==The Ultimate Fighter Nations Finale: Bisping vs. Kennedy==\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=The Ultimate Fighter Nations Finale: Bisping vs. Kennedy\n|image=TUF Nations Finale event poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date=April 16, 2014\n|venue=[[Colisée Pepsi]]\n|city=[[Quebec City, Quebec|Quebec City, Quebec, Canada]]\n|attendance=5,029{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/tuf-nations-finales-attendance-mark-pushes-live-gate-to-340000/|title=TUF Nations Finale's attendance mark pushes live gate to $340,000|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2014-04-16|access-date=2014-04-16}}\n|gate=$340,000\n|previousevent = [[UFC Fight Night: Nogueira vs. Nelson]]\n|followingevent = [[UFC on Fox: Werdum vs. Browne]]\n}}\n\n'''''The Ultimate Fighter Nations Finale: Bisping vs. Kennedy''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night: Bisping vs. Kennedy''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on April 16, 2014, at [[Colisée Pepsi]] in [[Quebec City, Quebec|Quebec City, Quebec, Canada]].\n\n===Background===\nThe event was headlined by a middleweight bout between [[Michael Bisping]] and [[Tim Kennedy (fighter)|Tim Kennedy]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/01/michael-bisping-cleared-for-tuf-nations-finale-headliner-with-tim-kennedy/|title=Michael Bisping cleared for TUF Nations Finale headliner with Tim Kennedy|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2014-01-30|access-date=2014-01-30}} Also featured on the card were the welterweight and middleweight finals of The Ultimate Fighter Nations: Canada vs. Australia and the coaches bout between [[Patrick Côté (fighter)|Patrick Côté]] and [[Kyle Noke]].\n\n[[Steve Bossé]] was expected to make his UFC debut against [[Ryan Jimmo]] at this event. However, Bossé was forced to pull out of the bout citing an injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/steve-bosse-out-at-tuf-nations-finale-promotion-seeks-new-opponent-for-ryan-jimmo/|title=Bosse out at TUF Nations Finale, promotion seeks new opponent for Jimmo|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|access-date=2014-03-05|date=2014-03-05}} He was replaced by UFC newcomer [[Sean O'Connell (fighter)|Sean O'Connell]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2014/3/6/5479302/ufc-signs-sean-oconnell-vs-ryan-jimmo-steve-bosse-injury-mma-news|title=Report: UFC targeting Sean O'Connell to replace Steve Bosse at TUF Nations Finale|publisher=bloodyelbow|author=Zane Simon|access-date=2014-03-06|date=2014-03-06}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Alex Caceres]] and [[Erik Pérez]] was briefly linked to this event,{{cite web |url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2014/2/21/5435036/mma-news-alex-caceres-vs-erik-perez-tuf-nations-finale-roop-vs-kimura|title=Alex Caceres vs. Erik Perez targeted for TUF Nations Finale|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander |access-date=2014-04-09|date=2014-02-21}} but never materialized.\n\n[[Shayna Baszler]] was originally scheduled to make her UFC debut on this card against [[Sarah Kaufman]]. However, she had to pull out of the bout due to injury and was briefly replaced by [[Amanda Nunes]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2014/4/3/5579176/amanda-nunes-replaces-shayna-baszler-meets-sarah-kaufman-at-tuf|title=Amanda Nunes replaces Shayna Baszler, meets Sarah Kaufman at TUF Nations Finale|publisher=MMA Fighting|author=Guilherme Cruz|access-date=2014-04-03|date=2014-04-03}} Five days later, Nunes also pulled out of the bout with a thumb injury.{{cite web|url=http://fansided.com/2014/04/08/amanda-nunes-injured-bout-sarah-kaufman/#!Dee6S|title=Amanda Nunes injured; out of bout with Sarah Kaufman |publisher=fansided.com|author=Josh Sanchez|access-date=2014-03-08|date=2014-03-08}} Kaufman instead fought [[Leslie Smith (fighter)|Leslie Smith]] in a rematch from their bout at [[Invicta FC events#Invicta FC 5: Penne vs. Waterson|Invicta FC 5: Penne vs. Waterson]]{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2014/4/8/5594210/ufc-amanda-nunes-sarah-kaufman-injured-tuf-nations-finale-bout-ufc-news|title=Amanda Nunes out, Leslie Smith in TUF Nations Finale bout with Sarah Kaufman|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Karim Zidan|access-date=2014-04-08|date=2014-04-08}} which ended in a controversial split decision.\n\n[[Evan Dunham]] was expected to face [[Mark Bocek]] at the event. However, Dunham pulled out of the bout in the week leading up to the event with an undisclosed injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/lightweight-evan-dunham-out-at-tuf-nations-finale-promotion-seeks-replacement-for-mark-bocek/|title=Evan Dunham out at TUF Nations Finale, promotion seeks replacement for Mark Bocek|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan and Matt Erickson|access-date=2014-04-07|date=2014-04-07}} Bocek instead faced UFC newcomer [[Mike De La Torre]] at the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2014/4/8/5594608/mark-bocek-draws-mike-de-la-torre-for-tuf-nations-finale-with-evan|title=Mark Bocek Draws Mike De La Torre For TUF Nations Finale With Evan Dunham Hurt |publisher=mmamania.com |author=Jesse Holland|access-date=2014-04-09|date=2014-04-09}}\n\nAn expected lightweight fight between [[Sam Stout]] and [[K. J. Noons]] was changed to a welterweight match up the week of the fight per the request of the fighters.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/k-j-noons-vs-sam-stouts-tuf-nations-finale-clash-changed-to-welterweight-contest/|title=K. J. Noons vs. Sam Stout's TUF Nations Finale clash changed to welterweight contest|publisher=mmajunkie.com|access-date=2014-04-14|date=2014-04-14}}\n\n===Results===\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Fight card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Tim Kennedy (fighter)|Tim Kennedy]]\n|def.\n|[[Michael Bisping]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (49–46, 49–46, 50–45)\n|5\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Patrick Côté (fighter)|Patrick Côté]]\n|def.\n|[[Kyle Noke]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–28, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[Elias Theodorou]]\n|def.\n|[[Sheldon Westcott]]\n|TKO (punches and elbows)\n|2\n|4:41\n|The Ultimate Fighter Nations Middleweight Final\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Chad Laprise]]\n|def.\n|[[Olivier Aubin-Mercier]]\n|Decision (split) (28–29, 29–28, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|The Ultimate Fighter Nations Welterweight Final\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Dustin Poirier]]\n|def.\n|[[Akira Corassani]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|2\n|0:42\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary card (Fox Sports 1)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[K. event.\n\nUFC President [[Dana White]] initially announced that a bout between Gustafsson and [[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]] would be headlining the event.{{cite just 5 days after the fight was announced news came that Nogueira had to withdraw from the bout due to an injury.{{cite reports came that said that Nogueira had never agreed to take the fight against Gustafsson at all.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/11/10/ufc-boss-reveals-antonio-rogerio-nogueira-never-agreed-to-face-gustafsson/|title=UFC boss reveals: Antonio Rogerio Nogueira never agreed to face Pearson]] was expected to face [[Melvin Guillard]] in a rematch at the event. Night 37|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=23 January 2014 |access-date=January 23, 2014}}\n\n[[Brad Pickett]] was expected to make his Flyweight debut against [[Ian McCall (fighter)|Ian McCall]] on this card.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/11/8/5078624/brad-pickett-dropping-down-to-ufc-flyweight-division-mma|title=Brad Pickett drops down to flyweight, wants fight at UFC by promotional newcomer [[Neil Seery]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/replacement-neil-seery-meets-brad-pickett-at-ufc-fight-night-37/|title=Irish newcomer Neil Seery meets Brad Pickett at UFC Fight Night 37 in London|publisher=MMA Junkie| author=Staff |date=2014-02-20 |access-date=2014-02-20}}\n\nA bout between [[Roland Delorme]] and [[Davey Grant]] was cancelled after the weigh ins the day before the event due to Grant suffering a torn meniscus.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/saturdays-ufc-fight-night-37-lineup-loses-a-bout/|title=Saturday's UFC Fight Night 37 lineup Mousasi]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 169: Barão vs. Faber 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on February 1, 2014, at the [[Prudential Center]] in [[Newark, New Jersey]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/ufcs-annual-super-bowl-weekend-card-shifts-from-las-vegas-to-newark-in-2014|title=UFC's annual Super Bowl weekend card shifts from Las Vegas to Newark in 2014|work=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-05-13|access-date=2013-05-13|archive-date=2013-07-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130708054302/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/07/ufcs-annual-super-bowl-weekend-card-shifts-from-las-vegas-to-newark-in-2014|url-status=dead}}\n\nThe event was released on DVD on May 6, 2014. The DVD was distributed by the label ''[[Anchor Bay Entertainment]]''.\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between the current champion [[Jon Jones]] and #1 contender [[Glover Teixeira]] was briefly linked as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jon-jones-glover-teixeira-mir-overeem-top-ufc-169-on-super-bowl-weekend|title=Jon Jones-Glover Teixeira, Mir-Overeem top UFC 169 on Super Bowl weekend|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=3 October 2013|access-date=2013-10-03|archive-date=2013-10-03|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131003234646/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jon-jones-glover-teixeira-mir-overeem-top-ufc-169-on-super-bowl-weekend|url-status=dead}} However, just days after the announcement, [[Dana White]] indicated that \"the timing didn't work out\" for the pairing and that fight would take place at a later event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jon-jones-glover-teixeira-title-fight-no-longer-on-ufc-169-super-bowl-card|title=Jon Jones-Glover Teixeira title fight no longer on UFC 169 Super Bowl card|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson and John Morgan|date=7 October 2013|access-date=2013-10-07|archive-date=2013-10-07|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20131007213316/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jon-jones-glover-teixeira-title-fight-no-longer-on-ufc-169-super-bowl-card|url-status=dead}}\n\nThe event was expected to be headlined by the long awaited return of the [[List of UFC champions#Bantamweight Championship|Bantamweight Champion]] [[Dominick Cruz]]. However, on January 6, UFC President Dana White announced that Cruz had torn his groin in training and pulled from the fight. Cruz had also vacated his title, making [[Renan Barão]] the undisputed bantamweight champion. Stepping up to replace Cruz was [[Urijah Faber]], in a rematch of their interim title bout at [[UFC 149]].{{cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/10252465/dominick-cruz-ufc-169-groin-injury-vacates-title|title=Dominick Cruz withdraws from fight|author=Staff|date=6 January 2014 |access-date=2014-01-06}}\n\nCo-featured on the card was [[List of UFC champions#Featherweight Championship|UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[José Aldo]],{{cite web |url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jose-aldo-vs-ricardo-lamas-dominick-cruz-vs-renan-barao-set-for-ufc-169 |title=Jose Aldo vs. Ricardo Lamas, Dominick Cruz vs. Renan Barão set for UFC 169 |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |date=October 29, 2013 |access-date=October 30, 2013 |archive-date=October 30, 2013 |archive-url=https://archive.today/20131030095335/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/10/jose-aldo-vs-ricardo-lamas-dominick-cruz-vs-renan-barao-set-for-ufc-169 |url-status=dead }} who defended his title against top contender [[Ricardo Lamas]].\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Frank Mir]] and [[Alistair Overeem]], scheduled to take place at [[UFC 167]], was moved to this event.{{cite web |url=https://www.espn.com/new-york/story/_/id/9766356/frank-mir-alistair-overeem-fight-moves-ufc-169-newark |title=Mir-Overeem fight moved to Feb. 1|publisher=espn.go..com |author=Brett Okamoto|date=2013-10-03|access-date=2013-10-03}}\n\n[[Bobby Green (fighter)|Bobby Green]] was expected to face [[Abel Trujillo]] at this event. However, Green pulled out of the fight for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by former WEC lightweight champion [[Jamie Varner]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2013/12/abel-trujillo-gets-new-opponent-for-ufc-169-main-card/|title=Abel Trujillo gets new opponent for UFC 169 main card|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2013-12-31|access-date=2013-12-31|archive-date=2014-01-01|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140101132028/http://mmajunkie.com/2013/12/abel-trujillo-gets-new-opponent-for-ufc-169-main-card/|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Chris Cariaso]] was expected to face [[Kyoji Horiguchi]] at the event. However, Horiguchi pulled out of the bout citing injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/01/kyoji-horiguchi-drops-off-ufc-169-card/|title=Kyoji Horiguchi drops off UFC 169 card|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Dann Stupp|date=2014-01-15|access-date=2014-01-15|archive-date=2014-01-16|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140116121725/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/01/kyoji-horiguchi-drops-off-ufc-169-card/|url-status=dead}} He was replaced by WEC veteran [[Danny Martinez]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/01/danny-martinez-gets-call-for-octagon-debut-at-ufc-169/|title=Danny Martinez gets call for octagon debut at UFC 169|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|author=Dann submission\n\n==Records set==\nThis event, along with [[UFC Fight Night: Machida vs. Mousasi]], [[UFC Fight Night: Silva vs. Bisping]], [[UFC Fight Night: Werdum vs. Tybura]], [[UFC Fight Night: Shogun vs. Smith]] and later [[UFC on ESPN: dos Anjos vs. Edwards]], [[UFC Fight Night: Vieira vs. Tate]] and [[UFC on ABC: Hill vs. Rountree Jr.]] is currently tied for the second most decisions in a UFC event at 10, previously beating [[UFC 161]]. [[UFC 263]] currently holds the record for most decisions in a UFC event at 11.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-263-post-event-facts-new-record-most-fight-time-single-event-company-history|title=UFC 263 post-event facts: Decision-heavy card shatters record for most fight time|author=Mike Bohn|date=June 13, 2021|work=[[MMAjunkie.com]]}}\n\nThis event broke the record for the longest fight time with 2:51:14, later broken also by [[UFC 263]] with 3:19:32.\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2014 in UFC]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist}}\n{{UFC Events}}\n\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship flagship events]]\n[[Category:2014 in [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on April 19, 2014, at the [[Amway Center]] in [[Orlando, Florida]].\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by a heavyweight number one contenders bout between [[Fabrício Werdum]] and [[Travis Browne]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/01/travis-browne-vs-fabricio-werdum-official-for-ufc-on-fox-11-main-event/|title=Travis Browne vs. Fabrício Werdum official for UFC on FOX 11 main event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|access-date=2014-01-30|date=2014-01-30}}\n\n[[Sean Soriano]] was expected to face Estevan Payan at this event. However, Soriano was forced to pull out due to injury and was replaced by [[Mike Brown (fighter)|Mike Brown]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/sean-soriano-out-mike-brown-meets-estevan-payan-at-ufc-on-fox-11/|title=Sean Soriano out, Mike Brown meets Estevan Payan at UFC on FOX 11|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|access-date=2014-02-26|date=2014-02-26|archive-date=2015-03-10|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20150310161238/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/sean-soriano-out-mike-brown-meets-estevan-payan-at-ufc-on-fox-11|url-status=dead}} In turn, Brown was forced out of the bout and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Alex White (fighter)|Alex White]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/mike-brown-out-estevan-payan-now-meets-newcomer-alex-white-at-ufc-on-fox-11/|title=Mike Brown out, Estevan Payan now meets newcomer Alex White at UFC on FOX 11|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=MMAJunkie.com Staff|access-date=2014-04-04|date=2014-04-04|archive-date=2014-04-04|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140404234431/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/mike-brown-out-estevan-payan-now-meets-newcomer-alex-white-at-ufc-on-fox-11/|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Amanda Nunes]] was expected to face UFC newcomer Alexis Dufresne on this card,{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/amanda-nunes-vs-alexis-dufresne-added-to-ufc-on-fox-11/|title=Amanda Nunes vs. Alexis Dufresne added to UFC on FOX 11 11 |publisher=mmajunkie.com |author=MMAJunkie.com Staff|access-date=2014-04-15|date=2014-03-23}} but the bout was cancelled when Dufresne was injured.\n\n[[Santiago Ponzinibbio]] was expected to face [[Jordan Mein]] at this event. However, Ponzinibbio was removed from the bout and was replaced by Hernani Perpetuo.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmatorch.com/artman2/publish/UFC_2/article_20472.shtml|title=Jordan Mein gets new opponent for UFC on Fox 11 in Hernani Perpétuo|publisher=mmatorch.com |author=Jamie Penick|access-date=2014-02-27|date=2014-02-27}}\n\n[[Josh Samman]] was expected to face [[Caio Magalhães]] at the event. However, on April 8, Samman pulled out of the bout citing a hamstring injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Luke Zachrich]].{{cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2014/4/9/5597814/caio-magalhaes-vs-luke-zachrich-booked-ufc-on-fox-11-josh-samman-hurt-mma|title=Caio Magalhaes vs. Luke Zachrich booked for UFC on FOX 11 with Josh Samman hurt |publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|access-date=2014-04-09|date=2014-04-09}}\n\nThe event had an average of 2.5 million viewers with 3.3 million viewers during the main event.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2014/4/22/5641146/ufc-on-fox-11-does-2-5-million-viewers|title=UFC on FOX 11 does 2.5 million viewers|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Meltzer|access-date=2014-04-22|date=2014-04-22}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Fabrício Werdum]]\n|def.\n|[[Travis Henderson 2","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2014}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC Fight Night: Shogun vs. Henderson 2\n|image=UFN 38 event poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2014|03|23}}\n|venue= [[Ginásio Nélio Dias]]\n|city= [[Natal, Brazil]]\n|attendance=6,828{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/ufc-fight-night-38-draws-reported-6828-attendance/|title=UFC Fight Night 38 draws reported 6,828 attendance|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-03-23|date=2014-03-23|archive-date=2023-04-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230408194357/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/ufc-fight-night-38-draws-reported-6828-attendance|url-status=dead}}\n|buyrate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC 171|UFC 171: Hendricks vs. Lawler]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Nogueira vs. Nelson]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Shogun vs. Henderson 2''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 38''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held on March 23, 2014, at the [[Ginásio Nélio Dias]] in [[Natal, Brazil]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/01/ufc-fight-night-38-shogun-vs-henderson-official-for-march-23-in-natal-brazil/|title='UFC Fight Night 38: Shogun vs. Henderson' official for March 23 in Natal, Brazil|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=22 January 2014|access-date=2014-01-22|archive-date=2014-01-22|archive-url=https://archive.today/20140122144701/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/01/ufc-fight-night-38-shogun-vs-henderson-official-for-march-23-in-natal-brazil/|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was headlined by the anticipated rematch between mixed martial arts legends [[Maurício Rua]] and [[Dan Henderson]]. Henderson won their first encounter back in November 2011 at [[UFC 139]] via unanimous decision in a bout lauded by many MMA pundits as the ''Fight of the Year'' for 2011 and one of the best bouts in the history of the UFC.{{cite web |url=https://sports.yahoo.com/mma/blog/cagewriter/post/UFC-139-all-timer-Henderson-wins-25-minute-clas?urn=mma-wp9754|title=UFC all-timer: Henderson wins 25-minute classic against Rua at UFC 139|publisher=yahoo.com |date=November 19, 2011 |access-date=November 20, 2011}}{{cite web |url=http://latimesblogs.latimes.com/sports_blog/2011/11/ufc-139-dana-white-says-henderson-vs-rua-one-of-best-fights-ever.html|title=Dana White on UFC 139: Henderson vs. Rua one of best fights ever|work=[[Los Angeles Times]] |date=November 19, 2011 |access-date=November 20, 2011}}\n\nThe co-main event featured a long-planned bout between [[Cezar Ferreira]] and [[C. B. Dollaway]]. The two were originally scheduled to meet on May 18, 2013 at [[UFC on FX 8]].{{cite web|url=http://portaldovaletudo.com.br/br/noticias/item/4409-roger-gracie-e-cezar-mutante-refor%C3%A7am-card-do-ufc-em-jaragu%C3%A1|title=Roger Gracie e Cezar Mutante reforçam card do UFC em Jaraguá (Brazilian Portuguese)|publisher=portaldovaletudo.com.br|date=2013-03-04|access-date=2013-03-04|author=Staff|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://archive.today/20130407132802/http://portaldovaletudo.com.br/br/noticias/item/4409-roger-gracie-e-cezar-mutante-refor%C3%A7am-card-do-ufc-em-jaragu%C3%A1|archive-date=2013-04-07}} However, both Dollaway{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/chris-camozzi-in-for-injured-c-b-dollaway-vs-cezar-ferreira-at-ufc-on-fx-8|title=Chris Camozzi in for injured C.B. Dollaway vs. Cezar Ferreira at UFC on FX 8|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=April 1, 2013|access-date=March 17, 2014|archive-date=April 3, 2013|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130403234320/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/chris-camozzi-in-for-injured-c-b-dollaway-vs-cezar-ferreira-at-ufc-on-fx-8|url-status=dead}} and Ferreira{{cite web|url=http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/chris-camozzi-vs-rafael-natal-slated-for-ufc-on-fx-8-in-brazil|title=Chris Camozzi vs. Rafael Natal slated for UFC on FX 8 in Brazil|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2013-04-07|access-date=2013-04-07|archive-date=2013-04-09|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20130409081332/http://www.mmajunkie.com/news/2013/04/chris-camozzi-vs-rafael-natal-slated-for-ufc-on-fx-8-in-brazil|url-status=dead}} had been forced from the bout due to injury.\n\n[[Gleison Tibau]] was expected to face [[Mairbek Taisumov]] at the event. However, Tibau pulled out of the bout citing injury and was replaced by [[Michel Prazeres]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/gleison-tibau-out-mairbek-taisumov-gets-new-ufc-fight-night-38-opponent/|title=Gleison Tibau out, Mairbek Taisumov gets new UFC Fight Night 38 opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=19 February 2014 |access-date=2014-02-19}}\n\n[[Diego Brandao]] was expected to face Will Chope at the event and both men weighed in successfully on March 22 for the fight. However, after the weigh ins, the bout was cancelled when documents were released that showed Chope assaulted and threatened his ex-wife, which led to jail time and a dishonorable discharge from the U.S. Air Force.{{cite web|url=http://msn.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-fighter-was-discharged-from-u-s-air-force-for-assaulting-wife-repeatedly-report-032314|title=UPDATE: Will Chope-Diego Brandao fight canceled after Chope abuse report goes public|publisher=msn.foxsports.com|date=March 23, 2014}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Ronny Markes]] weighed in at 190 pounds, 4 pounds over the middleweight non-title fight limit. His bout proceeded at catchweight and he was fined 20% of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Thiago Santos (fighter)|Thiago Santos]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/03/ufc-fight-night-38-live-and-official-weigh-in-results/|title=UFC Fight Night 38 weigh-in results|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-03-22|date=2014-03-22}}\n\nThe card saw an average of 936,000 viewers with a peak of 1.25 million during the main event.{{cite web|url=http://mmanuts.com/news/ufc-scores-solid-ratings-sundays-ufc-fight-night/ |title=UFC Scores Solid Ratings For Sunday's UFC Fight Night |publisher=mmanuts.com|author=Dave Walsh |date=March 25, 2014 |access-date=March 25, 2014 }}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Dan Henderson]]\n|def.\n|[[Maurício Rua]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|3\n|1:31\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Middleweight\n|[[C. heavyweight bout between [[Roy Nelson (fighter)|Roy Nelson]] and [[Antônio Rodrigo Nogueira]] headlined this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-abu-dhabi-april11|title=UFN: Abu Dhabi |publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-02-25|date=2014-02-25}}\n\nThe event was the second that the organization hosted in Abu Dhabi, after [[UFC 112]] in 2010. The event was also the first UFC and MMA event held at the [[Emirates Integrated Telecommunications Company#du Arena|du Arena]].\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Alptekin Özkiliç]] and [[Dustin Ortiz]] was expected to take place on this card. However, on April 3, the fight was cancelled due to injury to Özkiliç.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/alptekin-ozkilic-out-of-ufc-fight-night-39-bout-with-dustin-ortiz/|title=Alptekin Ozkilic out of UFC Fight Night 39 bout with Dustin Ortiz|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|access-date=2014-04-03|date=2014-04-03|archive-date=2014-04-05|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140405010801/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/alptekin-ozkilic-out-of-ufc-fight-night-39-bout-with-dustin-ortiz/|url-status=dead}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Chris Camozzi]] and [[Andrew Craig]] was expected to take place on this card, however the fight was cancelled on the day of the weigh-ins as Craig was ruled out with an illness. Despite the cancellation, the UFC announced that both fighters would receive their show money for the bout.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/illness-scraps-andrew-craig-vs-chris-camozzi-at-ufc-fight-night-39/|title=Illness scraps Andrew Craig vs. Chris Camozzi at UFC Fight Night martial arts]] event held on May 10, 2014, at the [[U.S. Bank Arena]] in [[Cincinnati, Ohio]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/ufc-returns-to-cincinnati-for-ufc-fight-fight-night-event-on-may-10/|title=UFC returns to Cincinnati for UFC Fight Night event on May 10|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2014-02-18|date=2014-02-18|archive-date=2016-05-27|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20160527025236/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/02/ufc-returns-to-cincinnati-for-ufc-fight-fight-night-event-on-may-10/|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second time that the organization has held an event in Cincinnati, after [[UFC 77]] back in 2007.\n\n[[Alexander Yakovlev (fighter)|Alexander Yakovlev]] and [[Yan Cabral]] were originally scheduled to face each other in a welterweight bout. However, on April 9, Yakovlev was moved to [[The Ultimate Fighter: Brazil 3#The Ultimate Fighter Brazil 3 Finale: dos Santos vs. Miocic|The Ultimate Fighter Brazil 3 Finale]] card as he replaced an injured [[Mike Pierce]] against [[Demian Maia]].{{cite news |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/replacement-alexander-yakovlev-meets-demian-maia-at-ufc-fight-night-42/ |title=Replacement Alexander Yakovlev meets Demian Maia at UFC Fight Night 42 |publisher=MMAjunkie.com |author=Staff |date=April 7, 2014 |archive-date=April 11, 2014 |access-date=April 10, 2014 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140411205956/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/replacement-alexander-yakovlev-meets-demian-maia-at-ufc-fight-night-42/ |url-status=dead }} Cabral instead faced [[Zak Cummings]] at the event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/zak-cummings-meets-yan-cabral-at-ufc-fight-night-40-in-cincinnati/|title=Zak Cummings meets Yan Cabral at UFC Fight Night 40 in Cincinnati|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=April 10, 2014}}{{Dead link|date=January 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot }}\n\n[[William Macario]] was expected to face [[Neil Magny]] at the event. However, Macario was removed from the bout for undisclosed reasons and replaced by returning veteran [[Tim Means]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/tim-means-replaces-william-macario-meets-neil-magny-at-ufc-fight-night-40/|title=Tim Means replaces William Macario, meets Neil Magny at UFC Fight Night 40|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=April 10, 2014|archive-date=April 11, 2014|access-date=April 11, 2014|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20140411054808/http://mmajunkie.com/2014/04/tim-means-replaces-william-macario-meets-neil-magny-at-ufc-fight-night-40/|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Anthony Lapsley]] initially weighed in at 174 pounds, over the welterweight limit allowance of 171 pounds. Lapsley was given an additional two hours to lose the weight. He successfully weighed in at 171 pounds two hours later.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/05/ufc-fight-night-40-live-and-official-weigh-in-results|title=UFC Fight Night 40 weigh-in results|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=May 9, 2014}}\n\nThis card featured the second most victories by fighters considered betting underdogs with eight, second only to [[UFC Fight Night 38]] that featured eight underdog wins.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Welterweight\n|[[Matt Brown (fighter)|Matt Brown]]\n|def.\n|[[Erick received $50,000 bonuses:{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/05/ufc-fight-night-40-bonuses-matt-brown-scoops-up-100k|title=UFC Fight Night 40 bonuses: Matt Brown scoops up Eduardo'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the Ohio Athletic Commission. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2014/05/ufc-fight-night-40-salaries-matt-brown-leads-680000-payroll|title=UFC Fight Night 40 salaries: Matt Brown leads $680,000 payroll|publisher=MMAjunkie.com|date=May 12, 2014|access-date=May 12, 2014|author=Matt Erickson}}\n* '''Matt Brown:''' $82,000 (includes $41,000 win bonus) def. Erick Silva: $22,000\n* '''Costas Philippou:''' $46,000 (includes $23,000 win bonus) def. Lorenz Larkin: $28,000\n* '''Daron Cruickshank:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Erik Koch: $18,000\n* '''Neil Magny:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Tim Means: $10,000\n* '''Soa Palelei:''' $32,000 (includes $16,000 win bonus) def. Ruan Potts: $10,000\n* '''Chris Cariaso:''' $42,000 (includes $21,000 win bonus) def. Louis Smolka: $10,000\n* '''Ed Herman:''' $80,000 (includes $40,000 win bonus) def. Rafael Natal: $26,000\n* '''Kyoji Horoguchi:''' $20,000 (includes $10,000 win bonus) def. Darrel Montague: $8,000\n* '''Zak Cummings:''' $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def. Yan Cabral: $10,000\n* '''Johnny Eduardo:''' $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def. Eddie Wineland: $21,000\n* '''Nik Lentz:''' $58,000 (includes $29,000 win bonus) def. Manvel Gamburyan: $25,000\n* '''Justin Salas:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Ben Wall: $8,000\n* '''Albert Tumenov:''' $16,000 (includes $8,000 win bonus) def.
